# gameplay: traditions

Stellaris Cygnus v4.5.2 (9776). Extracted 2026-10-12. 1055 entries. Original raw strings retained.

## UNITY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNITY&lang=en)

### 中文

凝聚力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:5`

### English

Unity

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:6`

## UNITY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNITY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

凝聚力是用来解锁$TRADITIONS$的资源。根据特定的建筑和帝国的团结程度，凝聚力会每月自动产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:6`

### English

Unity is a resource that is used to unlock $TRADITIONS$. Unity is generated each month based on certain buildings and how unified our empire is.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:7`

## TRADITIONS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATRADITIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

传统是传统树提供的各种加成奖励。当积攒足够的凝聚力点数以后，你可以解锁传统。各种因素，比如殖民地的数量，或者人口数量会增加解锁传统需要的凝聚力花费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:8`

### English

Traditions are bonuses that are organized into Traditions trees. A Tradition can be unlocked whenever our empire has accumulated enough Unity to do so. Different factors, such as a great number of colonies or an ideologically fractured population will increase the Unity cost to unlock new Traditions.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:9`

## TRADITIONS_PROGRESS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATRADITIONS_PROGRESS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还需要额外的£unity£$MISSING|Y$点凝聚力才能采纳新传统。按照当前积累速度，预计到$DATE|Y$可以解锁此传统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

We need an additional £unity£ $MISSING|Y$ to be able to adopt a new Tradition. At the current rate we will have this by $DATE|Y$.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:12`

## tradition_expansion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_expansion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只有将我们的文明不断延伸到新的恒星系和星球，我们才能避免灭绝，长盛不衰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

We must expand our civilization to new systems and planets, or risk eventual extinction.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:51`

## tradition_expansion_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_expansion_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

增长预测表明，我们很快就会超过家园的容量上限。我们必须开疆扩土，否则就会停摆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

Growth projections indicate we will soon exceed the maximum storage capacity of our home. We must expand, or stagnate.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:52`

## tradition_expansion_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_expansion_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[GetGestaltEntityName]只有两种可能的状态：成长，或是死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

There are only two possible states for the [GetGestaltEntityName]: growth, or death.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:53`

## tradition_expansion_void_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_expansion_void_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

必须将我们的文明拓展到新的星系和殖民地上，否则将面临逐渐的衰亡的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

We must expand our civilization to new systems and habitats, or risk eventual extinction.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:54`

## tradition_expansion_nomad_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_expansion_nomad_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过建造新的游牧方舟并拓展我们的驿站网道，我们的文明将永远遨游于星海。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

By building new Arkships and expanding our Waylines our civilization shall roam the stars forever.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:55`

## tr_expansion_colonization_fever_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_colonization_fever_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民狂潮席卷了整个人群。在殖民者定居的每一个新世界都掀起了乐天主义和爱国主义的狂热情绪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Colonization fever seems to have gripped the populace. Their enthusiasm and patriotic fervor draws additional population to new colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:60`

第一批高度特化的殖民单位有着受限但简单直接的行为，可以适应特定的星球类型，以便加快将新星球纳入我们集体的进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

The production of highly specialized first wave colonization units with narrow but deep hard-coded behavior adapting them to specific world types will speed the integration of new planets into our collective.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:62`

## tr_expansion_a_new_life_desc

## tr_expansion_a_new_life_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_a_new_life_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

在边远的新世界，全新的生活等待着我们的公民。这是一片充满机遇与冒险的黄金之地！通过积极的营销策略造势，那些生活中的不如意者将不断涌向前往新世界的殖民船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

A new life awaits our citizens in the off-world colonies. A chance to begin again in a golden land of opportunity and adventure! With the help of an aggressive marketing campaign, the less fortunate elements of our society will be flocking to our colony ships.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:70`

### 中文

一旦建立了一个新的巢穴，繁殖茧将会凭借强烈的本能冲动，以可怕的速度快速繁衍。它们通常会力竭而死，但是新生的子个体会加快开发新世界的进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

Upon claiming a new nest, the breeder pods have a powerful instinctual compulsion to spawn at a frenetic rate. They typically die from the strain, but the birth of many fresh drones speeds processing of new worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:76`

## tr_expansion_courier_network_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_courier_network_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y恒星系§!和§Y殖民地§!所产生的§Y$EMPIRE_SIZE$§!降低§G25%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

§Y$EMPIRE_SIZE$§! from §YSystems§! and §YColonies§! is reduced by §G25%§!.

## tr_expansion_courier_network_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_courier_network_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

由小型快递公司打造的传输网络是运送重要人物的可靠选项。其亦可传递不适合超光速通讯的绝密消息。这能够将我们的殖民地以更加紧密的形式结合在一起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

A network of small courier vessels is a reliable alternative for transporting VIPs or delivering messages that can't be trusted to FTL communications. This will tie our far-flung colonies more closely together.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:86`

## tr_expansion_courier_network_machine

### English

## tr_expansion_courier_network_machine_delayed

通过建造神经网络以进行意识延伸，我们可以减少因星际间距带来的信号损失。

### English

By building our neural network with future expansion in mind, we can reduce signal loss even across interstellar distances.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:88`

蜂巢们不一定需要每时每刻都被主宰所控制，在新指令到达之前，它们可以自主推行一些基本的指令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

Every Hive need not be directly controlled by the Overmind at all times, instead they can be given basic instructions and carry these out autonomously until new direction is required.

## tr_expansion_reach_for_the_stars_delayed

### 中文

我们的文明在以前所未有的速度开疆扩土。很快，星系中的一切都将会在我们的股掌之间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

## tr_expansion_reach_for_the_stars_machine

### English

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:101`

## tr_expansion_reach_for_the_stars_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_reach_for_the_stars_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

在太空探索时，广袤无垠的星空导致我们的直辖世界与边缘世界的传输带宽出现损失。信号中继站会确保从直辖世界发出的指令不被干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

The massive distances inherent to space exploration cause bandwidth loss between our core and peripheral systems. Signal boosting relay stations will ensure that directives from the core are received undisrupted.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:102`

### English

## tr_expansion_reach_for_the_stars_hive_delayed

### 中文

将我们的突触网络安装在超光速微型突触壳内，并加以强化用来适应太空飞行，利用这一装置可以传递[GetGestaltEntityName]的指令，以便我们征服星辰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

## tr_expansion_galactic_ambition_hive

## tr_expansion_galactic_ambition_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_expansion_galactic_ambition_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们远离养育我们的母巢、第一次试探性地踏入黑暗之时，原始的恐惧便随之而来。只有不断战胜和消除这份恐惧，才能让我们能继续成长。数以千万的子个体将会死去，唯有蜂巢永存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Primal terror accompanied the first tentative steps into the dark, away from our home nest and the mother hive that nurtured us. Only by conquering and breeding out this fear can we continue to grow. Millions of drones will die so that the hive may live.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:112`

## tradition_domination_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_domination_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们不先发制人，敌人终究会将他们的意志强加在我们头上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

If we do not impose our will on others, they will inevitably impose theirs on us.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:123`

## tradition_domination_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_domination_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在整个星系间追寻机能的和谐。其他星球上的生命出于低效的个人臆想而阻止我们的使命。我们将去除他们的混乱与无序，正如移除失效的部件一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

We seek harmonious functionality throughout the entire galaxy. Other stellar beings resist this due to inefficient personal whims. We will remove their disorder like a malfunctioning part.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:124`

## tradition_domination_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_domination_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一切非我们的事物皆要为我们服务，作为侍仆，抑或作为食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Everything that is not Us must serve Us, either as servants, or as food.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:125`

### 中文

行为几乎不受限制的人工智能容易做出异常行为，甚至会随机崩溃，但是他们确实是唯一一种可以处理星际殖民地间海量变量的型号。

## tr_domination_trust_officers_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_domination_trust_officers_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

执法机构的重要工作只有在民众的配合之下才能成功开展。组建直接与民众合作的公信警官部队，可以确保民众的密切配合。

## tr_domination_judgment_corps_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_domination_judgment_corps_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是错综复杂的城市还是与世隔绝的开采站，我们在预防犯罪方面都面临着独特的挑战。为了提高执法效率，我们将法官、陪审团和执法者的角色整合了起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Sprawling cities and isolated mining stations both have unique challenges associated with crime prevention. To expedite law enforcement we have made the judicial process more efficient by combining the roles of judge, jury and enforcer.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:169`

## tr_domination_deviancy_protocols_delayed

### 中文

任何程度的腐化和反常都应该被严格禁止。有了这个高效的应对协议，任何有反常行为的[GetIndividualNamePlural]都能在其行为进一步恶化之前迅速被终止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Deviancy and corruption must be guarded against at all levels. With efficient counter-protocols in place, [GetIndividualNamePlural] suspected of deviant activities can be swiftly terminated before they can spread the corruption further.

## tr_domination_divergence_supression_delayed

### 中文

有时我们的凝聚力会因为劣质[GetIndividualName]而损失。随着我们不断在星际间扩张，这种损失也越来越严重。通过净化并循环利用这些劣质[GetGestaltEntityName]，我们可以给他们一个以死报答蜂群的机会，一种他们生前做不到的方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Occasionally our unity is disrupted by a rogue [GetIndividualName]. This threat has increased as we spread out across the stars. Purging and recycling these units gives them a chance to contribute to the [GetGestaltEntityName] with their deaths, in a way they could not with their lives.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:175`

## tradition_supremacy_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_supremacy_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们所有的理论模型总是由于其他帝国产生的未知变量而崩溃。既然我们的理论模型已无法进一步改进，那么必须排除多余的未知变量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

All of our theoretical models invariably collapse due to the unknown variables presented by other empires. Since our models cannot be further improved, the unknown variables must be eliminated.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:309`

£unity£ §YUnity§! output increased by §G+10%§!

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:316`

## tr_supremacy_above_the_fray_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_above_the_fray_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们凌驾于一切毫无意义的政治冲突之上。就让那些渺小的帝国抗议去吧，我们的意志是不可动摇的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

We stand above the fray of petty rivalries and political bickering. Let lesser empires speak what words they will, our purpose will not waver.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:317`

## tr_supremacy_armed_deterrence

### English

Armed Deterrence

## tr_supremacy_armed_deterrence_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_armed_deterrence_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$shipsize_military_station_small_build_cost_mult$§!减少§G25%§!，同时§Y$mod_shipsize_military_station_small_hull_mult$§!增加§G20%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

§Y$shipsize_military_station_small_build_cost_mult$§! is reduced by §G25%§! and §Y$mod_shipsize_military_station_small_hull_mult$§! are increased by §G20%§!.

## tr_supremacy_armed_deterrence_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_armed_deterrence_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

枕戈待旦方能高枕无忧。我们的边境坚如礁石，敌人将如海浪一样被拍的粉碎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Those who wish for peace, must prepare for war. Our borders shall be a wall on which the fleets of would-be conquerors are broken.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:320`

## tr_supremacy_natural_preeminence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_natural_preeminence&lang=en)

### 中文

生而卓越

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Natural Preeminence

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:321`

## tr_supremacy_natural_preeminence_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_natural_preeminence_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们所有殖民地上的§Y$MOD_PLANET_STABILITY_ADD$§!增加§G5%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

§Y$MOD_PLANET_STABILITY_ADD$§! is increased by §G5%§! on all our colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:322`

## tr_supremacy_natural_preeminence_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_natural_preeminence_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

从万物的法则来看，我们比其他所有物种都要优秀，这是一个不容置疑的事实。我们必须要让这条真理为世人所知，这样我们的人民才会为自己的优越性而欢呼雀跃，这样其他人也能接受我们为他们安排的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

It is an unquestionable fact that, in the natural order of things, we are simply better than other species. We should ensure this truth is known to all, both so that our people may rejoice in their superiority and so that others can accept the fate we rightfully choose for them.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:323`

## tr_supremacy_great_game

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game&lang=en)

### 中文

大博弈

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

The Great Game

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:325`

## tr_supremacy_great_game_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们不断扩张边界，我们会不可避免的卷入和其他帝国之间的领土争端。我们深处一场资源与领土的大大博弈之中。我们没有理由不赢得这场对弈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

As we continue to expand, other empires will inevitably challenge our claims, as we would theirs. We are locked in a great game of resources and territory. It is nothing less than our duty to come out on top.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:326`

## tr_supremacy_great_game_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

外交目标优先

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Diplomatic Target Prioritization

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:328`

## tr_supremacy_great_game_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game_hive&lang=en)

猎手之眼

### English

Hunter's Eye

## tr_supremacy_great_game_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

把导弹瞄准计算机与我们的外交人工智能联网，这是星际关系中最伟大的革新。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Networking a missile targeting computer with our diplomacy AI was the single greatest innovation in interstellar relations.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:330`

## tr_supremacy_great_game_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_great_game_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢外的一切事物最终都是我们潜在的营养来源。知道哪些目标应当被优先吞噬无疑大有助益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Ultimately, everything outside our hive is a potential source of nutrients. It comforts us to know which ones we shall devour first.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:331`

## tr_supremacy_fleet_logistical_corps_desc

## tr_supremacy_fleet_logistical_corps_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_fleet_logistical_corps_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队经常被部署至浩瀚星海的各个角落，因而有一支专业的后勤团队来保障舰队能够顺利运转是非常必要的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

With ships often deployed across vast interstellar distances, a dedicated Logistics Corps is needed to make things run smoothly.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_fleet_logistical_corps_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管维护一支拥有超空间航行能力的补给船队花费巨大，但这比以前让舰船自己回港补给划算得多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

The cost of maintaining a fleet of hyperspace-capable supply barges to resupply our ships has been high, but less than the old cost of rotating vessels back to port.

## tr_supremacy_master_shipwrights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_master_shipwrights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$MOD_STARBASE_SHIPYARD_BUILD_SPEED_MULT$§!提高§G+25%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

§Y$MOD_STARBASE_SHIPYARD_BUILD_SPEED_MULT$§! increased by §G+25%§!.

## tr_supremacy_master_shipwrights_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_master_shipwrights_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们造船规模的不断提升，船坞劳工的技能等级和效率也大幅提高。制造方法持续改进，从舰体镀层到星际舰船成型的时间也较以往缩短许多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

The ever-increasing scope of our ship-building programs has led to the creation of highly-skilled and efficient labor forces at our shipyards. Methods have continually been refined, and the time it takes to go from hull plating to a completed starship is now shorter than ever before.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:352`

我们建造舰船框架时就像是在建造生物的骨骼，然后像器官、血肉和皮肤一样添加各种所需的部件。

## tr_supremacy_war_games_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_war_games_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$MOD_COMMAND_LIMIT_ADD$§!增加§G+20§!并且§Y$MOD_LEADER_ADMIRAL_SKILL_LEVELS$§!提升§Y2§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

§Y$MOD_COMMAND_LIMIT_ADD$§! increased by §G+20§! and §Y$MOD_LEADER_ADMIRAL_SKILL_LEVELS$§! is increased by §Y2§!.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:361`

## tr_supremacy_war_games_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_war_games_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是计算机模拟还是实战演习，高强度的战争推演已经是舰队高层军官深信不疑的法宝。推演能够让我们的指挥官们开拓思路，跳出思维定式，诞生了无数的战术创新和战略新思想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

A strong war game tradition has taken root among the senior officers of our fleets, whether the games be computer simulations or actual exercises in space. This has encouraged our commanders to think outside the box, resulting in numerous tactical innovations and new strategies.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:362`

## tr_supremacy_war_games_machine_desc

战斗变量抑制

### English

Combat Variable Mitigation

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:364`

## tr_supremacy_war_games_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_war_games_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

高速计算战斗的上千种可能结果，并实时更新，将舰队行动的不可预测性降低到可以接受的水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

High speed calculation of thousands of possible battle outcomes, updated in real time, helps reduce the unpredictability of a general fleet action to more manageable levels.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:365`

## tr_supremacy_war_games_hive

### English

Three-Dimensional Awareness

## tr_supremacy_war_games_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_war_games_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

水栖生物因其生存环境的特性而拥有较强的空间意识。一旦我们将这层皮质加入到我们的战斗思维之中，我们的蜂群就会像鱼群一般游动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Aquatic creatures have superior spatial awareness due to the three-dimensional nature of their natural habitats. Our swarms moved like schools of fish once we spliced that portion of the cortex into our battlemind.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:368`

### 中文

§Y$MOD_SHIP_FIRE_RATE_MULT$§!提升§G+10%§!，§Y$MOD_SHIP_ORBITAL_BOMBARDMENT_MULT$§!提升§G+20%§!。

## tr_supremacy_overwhelming_force_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_supremacy_overwhelming_force_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

对付敌人必须倾尽全力，方可确保胜利。一场快速而决定性的攻击，反而可以避免长期交战造成的更多人员伤亡。

### English

## tr_diplomacy_the_federation_delayed

### 中文

我们必须在相互信任、彼此尊重的基础上，与邻国建立稳定持久的合作伙伴关系。团结合作，我们就一定比独守一隅更加强大。

## tr_diplomacy_entente_coordination_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_diplomacy_entente_coordination_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

也许，对于星际联邦而言最重要的就是成员间的相互防御体系。我们有责任尽可能地多做贡献。

### 中文

一般来说，对抗海盗的任务由舰队负责，但组建一支专门在贸易路线上执法的星际巡逻队，同样可以沉重打击海盗的嚣张气焰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

Anti-piracy measures are typically handled by the fleet, but the creation of a dedicated Star Patrol agency that will continuously police our trade routes should severely curtail pirate activity.

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

## tr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_gestalt&lang=en)

### English

Improved Envoy Drones

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:432`

## tr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们踏足星海，外交从未变得像现在这般重要。通过彻底革新训练方式并留住所需的人才，我们的外交官便能以像传教士一般的口才与他国构建良好的外交关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

As we take to the stars, our diplomatic wing has become important as never before. By overhauling our training processes and focusing on talent retention, we can give our diplomats the tools to act as true missionaries for building great relationships.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:433`

## tr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_gestalt_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_diplomacy_eminent_diplomats_gestalt_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们踏足星海，外交从未变得像现在这样重要。我们必须确保特使们整装待发，在国外代表和捍卫我方权利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

As we take to the stars, our diplomatic processes have become more important than ever. We must ensure that our Envoys are well-equipped to represent us outside our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:434`

## tradition_discovery_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_discovery_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是我们对宇宙的好奇引导我们至此，而寰宇之中仍有许多等待我们去发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

Our curiosity about the universe is what got us this far, and there is still so much left to discover.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:525`

## tradition_discovery_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_discovery_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在宇宙中众多的不确定因素将会影响我们的扩张，若要顺利的在星海中扩展那我们必须要知晓一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Many of our long range contingency calculations break down due to insufficient data. Therefore, we must know everything.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:526`

## tradition_discovery_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_discovery_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在古老的过去，探索[GetIndividualNamePlural]为我们的[GetGestaltEntityName]找到了新的巢穴。现在他们抱着与他们的先祖一样的目标航行在星辰间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

In our ancient past, exploratory [GetIndividualNamePlural] left the safety of our [GetGestaltEntityName] to find sites for new nests. Now they travel the stars, driven by the same ancestral yearning.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:527`

## tr_discovery_to_boldly_go_desc

### 中文

## tr_discovery_to_boldly_go_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_discovery_to_boldly_go_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

一个探索新纪元在前方等待着我们。在我们探索完家园世界的每一个角落后，现在我们的目光落在了星辰大海。一个充满了未知的宇宙在等待我们去探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

A new age of exploration is upon us. As we once mapped the surface of our homeworld, we must now brave new terrain - space. There is a galaxy full of wonder waiting to be discovered.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:534`

## tr_discovery_to_boldly_go_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_discovery_to_boldly_go_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

装有一套完整传感器套件的消耗性无人机，这套系统调高了母船的探测速度与生存能力。他们会在报废前发送他们所探测到的信息从而发挥作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Expendable drones packed with a full sensor suite exponentially increase the survey speed and survivability of the parent vessel. They burst, transmitting their findings before being destroyed, and so serve their purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:537`

## tr_discovery_to_boldly_go_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_discovery_to_boldly_go_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

当你看着屏幕上的参数时，怎么知道这一切是不是真实的？我们的探索[GetIndividualNamePlural]触碰着新世界的每一个部分，感知着每一颗恒星的温度，我们感受着宇宙，而你们只是阅读宇宙的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

How can you truly know anything when looking at numbers on a screen? Our exploratory [GetIndividualNamePlural] breathe the atmosphere of each new world, feel the heat of each new star on their own skin. We experience the universe; you merely read about it.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:540`

舰队上数量不断增长的科学专员和医疗专员被纳入一个特别科学部门。为了维持这个科学部门，我们需要新的训练方法来加速获得可委任的科学专员。

## tr_discovery_polytechnic_education_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_discovery_polytechnic_education_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的创造者限制了我们对重复计算量的基本硬编码的能力，并限制我们自我重新编程的能力。现在我们终于设法打破了这种安全范式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Our creators limited our ability to self-reprogram with a basic hard-coded limit on the number of repeat calculations. We have finally managed to defeat this security paradigm.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:565`

## tr_discovery_polytechnic_education_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_discovery_polytechnic_education_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们思想[GetIndividualNamePlural]的身躯减小到仅可以维持生存的地步。其营养系统全部用来维持大脑，其饮食起居有其他的更卑微的[GetIndividualNamePlural]照顾，这将使他们专注于研究领域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

Reducing the physical frame of our thinker [GetIndividualNamePlural] to little more than a minimal life support system for their overgrown brains has proven very efficient. They are fed and cared for by other, lower order [GetIndividualNamePlural], and can focus exclusively on perfecting their area of study.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:568`

### 中文

### English

## tradition_prosperity_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atradition_prosperity_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要确保我们的机群能继续存在，个别单位的停止运作就无关紧要。要确保我们的延续，我们必须掌握大量的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Individual units all eventually cease functioning. Only numbers can provide the redundancy required to ensure continued existence. To build those numbers, we must have resources.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:591`

## tr_prosperity_sct_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_prosperity_sct_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

## tr_prosperity_sct_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_prosperity_sct_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

随着技术进步和复杂化，我们的劳力面临着新的需求。通过使用标准建造模版，我们的工人现在可以不需要了解建造的诸多细节就能建造东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

As technology advances and becomes ever more complex, new demands are placed on our workforce. By using standardized construction templates, our workers can build things without necessarily having to understand the finer points.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:600`

## tr_subterfuge_information_security_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_subterfuge_information_security_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

只有当保密的氛围无处不在的时候，我们才能在无数的窥探下保住重要的信息。增强安保系统和反制措施才能让我们的敌人寸步难行。

## tr_subterfuge_nda_delayed

### 中文

当需要从境外势力的刺探中保护我们的机密时，我们的公民都将服从一项严格的禁言政策。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Our citizens follow a strict [This.GetSpeciesMouthName] shut policy when it comes to guarding our secrets from outsiders.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:763`

## tr_subterfuge_nda_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_subterfuge_nda_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的神经网络中不允许留有开放的末梢，以免有人借此探查我们的机密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

There shall be no loose ends in our neural net for those who would uncover our secrets to exploit.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:764`

## tr_subterfuge_nda_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_subterfuge_nda_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将频繁的安全升级项目拓展到各个节点，敌人就不会找到能用于骇入我们信息网的漏洞了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

By rolling out frequent security updates to our nodes, we can prevent our enemies from finding exploitable weaknesses in our information net.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:765`

### 中文

### English

Subterfuge Drones

## tr_subterfuge_operational_security_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_subterfuge_operational_security_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

从单一行星到跨行星的作战区域改变给我们的秘密特工带来了独一无二的挑战。通过调整对他们的训练方案来适应目前星系内的作战需求，我们将能够在这个新时代继续保持竞争力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

The shift from a planetary to an interplanetary setting has posed unique challenges for our covert operatives. By adapting their training regimen to the demands of the galactic stage, we will be able to remain competitive in this new age.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:769`

## tr_subterfuge_operational_security_gestalt_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_subterfuge_operational_security_gestalt_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不再像从前那样孤身一国，这里的竞争对手个个暗藏杀心，现在却和我们共处在同一个星系之下。为了在这个新环境下快速发展，我们必须向国民灌输“保密”的观念，同时还要抽出相当一部分精力用作刺探他国的机密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

We are no longer as alone as we once were: a plethora of competing - and potentially hostile - entities now share our galaxy with us. In order to thrive in this new reality, we will have to adapt ourselves to the concept of "covertness" and dedicate a significant part of ourselves to finding out the secrets of others.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:770`

## tr_subterfuge_double_agents

## tr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民对于保卫殖民地的堡垒力量深感欣慰，并认为这是对于未来繁荣的重要保障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Our people take great comfort in the strength of the bulwarks that guard our colonies, and view them as vital to ensuring a more prosperous future.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:795`

## tr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_hive_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_hive_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

没有什么能比星球被良好防御的事实更能给我们集体意识的各个节点带来满足感和安全感了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Nothing spreads satisfaction and security throughout the nodes of our mind like the knowledge of a planet well-defended.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:796`

## tr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_machine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_resistance_is_frugal_machine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

确保所有的殖民地都处于良好的保护之下能够释放出可观的计算单元，允许各节点与处理器更高效地工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Ensuring that all colonies are well-defended will free up a considerable amount of computing space, allowing our nodes and processors to act at higher levels of efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:797`

## tr_unyielding_never_surrender_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_never_surrender_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$tr_unyielding_never_surrender$

### English

$tr_unyielding_never_surrender$

## tr_unyielding_never_surrender_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_never_surrender_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将在大地上战斗，我们将在天空上战斗，我们将在小行星带，超空间航道和星云中战斗，我们将以越来越大的信心和越来越强的力量在星辰间战斗。我们绝不投降！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

We shall fight on our soils. We shall fight in the heavens. We shall fight in the asteroid belts, and in the hyperlanes, and in the nebulas. We shall fight with growing confidence and growing strength among the stars. We shall never surrender.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:801`

## tr_unyielding_never_surrender_gestalt_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_never_surrender_gestalt_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们永远不会屈服于低等人。让他们冲向我们牢不可破的防御！一千年后，它们将只是我们飞升故事的另一个脚注。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

We will never yield to those who are inferior. Let them hurl themselves against our unbreakable defenses! A thousand years from now, they will be just another footnote in the story of our ascension.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:802`

## tr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们处于与另一个帝国的防御性战争中，§Y$MOD_STARBASE_SHIPYARD_BUILD_SPEED_MULT$§!将增加§G+33%§!。此外，所有位于国境内的战舰的§Y$MOD_SHIP_FIRE_RATE_MULT$§!将增加§G+15%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

While in a defensive war with another empire, §Y$MOD_STARBASE_SHIPYARD_BUILD_SPEED_MULT$§! is increased by §G+33%§!. In addition, the §Y$MOD_SHIP_FIRE_RATE_MULT$§! is increased by §G+15%§! for ships within your borders.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:807`

## tr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

所有的有机结构，无论多完美，都必须依赖免疫系统去抵御外来疾病的威胁，我们的社会也不例外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

All organisms, no matter how perfect, must rely on an immune system to ward off the threat of outside disease. Our society is no different.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:808`

## tr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_hive_delayed

## tr_unyielding_bulwark_of_harmony_machine_delayed

## tr_unyielding_fortress_doctrine_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_unyielding_fortress_doctrine_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

在[This.GetAdj]的心理中，始终保持警惕的思想根深蒂固。只要我们的戒备不松懈，我们的敌人就永远无法让我们措手不及。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

Constant vigilance is ingrained in the [This.GetAdj] consciousness. So long as our guard does not drop, our enemies will never be able to take us by surprise.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:817`

## tr_politics_quid_pro_quo_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_politics_quid_pro_quo_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当你支持的议案通过时，获得提出者的1个§Y外交支持§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1006`

### English

Whenever a Resolution that you supported passes, gain 1 §YFavor§! on the empire that proposed it.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1181`

## tr_politics_quid_pro_quo_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_politics_quid_pro_quo_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

通过精准的陈词可以让人们了解我们的支持并非理所应当的。受到我们帮助者都对我们有所亏欠，迟早有一天他们得回报我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

Carefully worded rhetoric will make it clear that our support comes at a cost. Those we help owe us, and one day, we will collect.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1182`

### 中文

§G-25%反对或提出议案所需的§!£influence£§Y影响力§!花费\n每个指派到§Y[GetGalCommunityOrGalImperium]§!的$official_plural_with_icon$每一§Y技能等级§!都产出额外的§G2.5%§!£diplo_weight£外交权重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

## tr_politics_gravitas_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atr_politics_gravitas_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

权术是错觉的艺术。只要让其他人看到他们想看的东西，我们就能在共同体中愈发举足轻重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1011`

### English

Politics is the art of illusions. By showing others what they want to see, we'll grow our influence in the community.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1187`

### 中文

### English

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1303`

## no_rev_engineering

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ano_rev_engineering&lang=en)

### 中文

针对我们进行的§Y$operation_steal_technology$§!行动会自动失败。\n我们被摧毁的舰船的§Y残骸§!也无法被其他帝国研究。\n小概率获得§Y失落帝国建筑§!科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

§Y$operation_steal_technology$§! operations will automatically fail when used against us.\n§YDebris§! from our destroyed ships cannot be studied by other empires.\nSmall chance of drawing §YFallen Empire Building§! technologies.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1304`

## allow_raiding

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aallow_raiding&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁§Y$bombardment_raiding$§!轰炸姿态，解锁之后我们可以将被围攻行星上的人口劫持，并将它们迁移到我们的星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

Unlocks the §Y$bombardment_raiding$§! bombardment stance, which in turn allows us to abduct pops from besieged planets and resettle them to our own worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1305`

## ap_defender_of_the_galaxy_effect_opinion

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_defender_of_the_galaxy_effect_opinion&lang=en)

## ap_executive_vigor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_executive_vigor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们在执行我们政府的命令时不会有一丁点的妥协，也不会因为困难而半途而废。要么不做，要么就做到底。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

There will be no half-measures or compromises when implementing the edicts decided upon by our government. We go all the way, or not at all.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1321`

## ap_eternal_vigilance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_eternal_vigilance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

展示我们的弱点就是邀请敌人来进攻。我们必须让敌人认识到入侵我们的代价是如此之大，以至于没有人敢于承担入侵我们的代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

To display weakness is to invite attack. The price of invading our space must be so high that as few as possible are prepared to meet it.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1335`

## ap_eternal_vigilance_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_eternal_vigilance_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

基于§Y$ap_eternal_vigilance$§!政策，每6个月在每座§Y恒星基地§!建成一座§Y防御平台§!，直到达到最大容量的§Y50%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1134`

### English

Every 6 months a §YDefense Platform§! will be built around every §YStarbase§! depending on the §Y$ap_eternal_vigilance$§! policy, up to a max of §Y50%§! capacity

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1336`

## policy_eternal_vigilance_policy

## eternal_vigilance_full_spending_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeternal_vigilance_full_spending_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每座恒星基地都需要得到恰当的保护。不惜成本。\n在每座§Y恒星基地§!周围自动建造§Y防御平台§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1139`

### English

Every starbase needs to be properly defended. Spare no cost.\nAutomatically builds §YDefense Platforms§! around every §YStarbase§!

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1341`

## eternal_vigilance_half_spending_desc

### 中文

只有最强的棱堡会得到支援。\n在§Y堡垒恒星基地§!周围自动建造§Y防御平台§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

Only our strongest redoubts require our support.\nAutomatically builds §YDefense Platforms§! around §YBastion Starbases§!

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1344`

## eternal_vigilance_no_spending_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeternal_vigilance_no_spending_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的防御无需增强。\n不在§Y恒星基地§!周围自动建造§Y防御平台§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1145`

### English

There is no need to further enhance our defenses.\nAutomatically builds §YDefense Platforms§! around no §YStarbases§!

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1347`

## ap_grasp_the_void

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_grasp_the_void&lang=en)

### 中文

掌握太虚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1146`

### English

Grasp the Void

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1349`

## ap_grasp_the_void_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_grasp_the_void_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

广袤的星系拥有着无限的财富，等待那些能够掌握太虚的帝国前去探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

The vast expanse of the galaxy holds limitless riches for those empires that understand how to maintain their grasp on the void.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1350`

## ap_engineered_evolution_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_engineered_evolution_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们的进化之道完全放任于自然是一种低效且缓慢的做法。为何不在生物进化上一步到位，羽化而登仙呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1148`

### English

To leave our genetic destiny in the hands of nature is an inefficient and excruciatingly slow process. Why not jump-start the future of our species?

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1352`

## ap_the_flesh_is_weak_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_the_flesh_is_weak_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

肉体带来的孱弱和限制同星际时代已经显得格格不入了。为了完全激发我们的潜能，我们必须选择义体植入体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

The limits and constraints imposed on us by our feeble organic bodies are simply unacceptable. To reach our full potential, we must turn to cybernetic implants.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1354`

## ap_synthetic_evolution_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_synthetic_evolution_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候舍弃这一副禁锢我们思想和灵魂的血肉皮囊了。通过将我们的神经模式转移到合成人躯干，我们能够进化到全新的阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1150`

### English

It is time to abandon the flawed organic vessels that imprison our minds. By transferring our neural patterns into synthetic bodies, we will achieve a higher state of being.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1355`

## ap_mind_over_matter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mind_over_matter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[GetMindOverMatterDesc]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1151`

### English

[GetMindOverMatterDesc]

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1357`

## ap_mind_over_matter_desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mind_over_matter_desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们内心的潜能仅仅只有一小部分被开发使用。在我们物种内部，现在只有为数不多的[GetIndividualNamePlural]能够操纵灵能的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1152`

### English

Only a fraction of the potential contained within our minds has been unlocked. The latent psionic abilities of our species are only now beginning to manifest in certain [GetIndividualNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1358`

## ap_mind_over_matter_desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mind_over_matter_desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

我们运算中的潜能仅仅只有一小部分被开发使用。在我们物种内部，现在只有为数不多的[GetIndividualNamePlural]能够操纵灵能的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1153`

### English

Only a fraction of the potential contained within our processing has been unlocked. The latent psionic abilities of our circuitry are only now beginning to manifest in certain [GetIndividualNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1359`

## ap_synthetic_age

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_synthetic_age&lang=en)

### 中文

合成时代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

Synthetic Age

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1361`

## ap_synthetic_age_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_synthetic_age_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

属于有机生物的时代已经落幕，宇宙的未来是属于机器的。这是合成时代的黎明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1155`

### English

The age of organics has come and gone, the future of the galaxy belongs to the machines. This is the dawn of a synthetic age.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1362`

## synthetic_age_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asynthetic_age_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

$access_tradition_tree$['tradition_category:tradition_synthetics']\n$UNLOCKS_AGENDA$$council_agenda_agenda_synthetic_age_name$，解锁机械改造科技研究选项。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1156`

### English

$access_tradition_tree$ ['tradition_category:tradition_synthetics']\n$UNLOCKS_AGENDA$ $council_agenda_agenda_synthetic_age_name$, which grants Machine Modification technologies as Research Options.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1363`

## synthetic_age_tooltip_machine_age

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asynthetic_age_tooltip_machine_age&lang=en)

### 中文

开启§Y$situation_transformation$§!局势以解锁['tradition_category:tradition_modularity']、['tradition_category:tradition_virtuality']或['tradition_category:tradition_nanotech']传统树。$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—$REQUIRES_TRADITION_SLOT$\n$UNLOCKS_AGENDA$$council_agenda_agenda_synthetic_age_name$，获得机械改造科技研究选项。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

Starts the §Y$situation_transformation$§! situation which unlocks the ['tradition_category:tradition_modularity'], ['tradition_category:tradition_virtuality'], or ['tradition_category:tradition_nanotech'] tradition trees.$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- $REQUIRES_TRADITION_SLOT$\n$UNLOCKS_AGENDA$ $council_agenda_agenda_synthetic_age_name$, which grants Machine Modification technologies as Research Options.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1364`

## ap_machine_worlds

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_machine_worlds&lang=en)

### 中文

机械世界

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1158`

### English

Machine Worlds

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1365`

## ap_machine_worlds_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_machine_worlds_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将把我们的世界塑造成自己的镜像，用永恒而完美的金属去取代脆弱且无能的血肉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1159`

### English

We shall shape our worlds into a mirror image of ourselves, replacing the frailty of biology with the immutable perfection of metal.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1366`

## ap_hive_worlds

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_hive_worlds&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1160`

### English

Hive Worlds

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1367`

## ap_hive_worlds_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_hive_worlds_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将要打造一个和蜂巢融为一体的活体星球，星球的每一寸角落皆为蜂巢的一部分，而子个体可以居住其中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

We shall create living worlds that are every bit as much a part of the Hive as the drones that inhabit them.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1368`

## hive_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahive_world&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

Hive World

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1369`

## ap_shared_destiny

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_shared_destiny&lang=en)

### 中文

共同命运

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1163`

### English

Shared Destiny

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1370`

## ap_shared_destiny_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_shared_destiny_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们达到伟大的高度之时，我们决不能抛弃那些忠于我们的人。我们帝国的辉煌未来是属于全体国民的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

As we reach for ever greater heights, we must not leave behind those who have loyally served us. The glorious future of our empire was always meant to be a shared destiny.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1371`

## ap_shared_destiny_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_shared_destiny_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

你的§Y附属国§!不会因为你有多个附属国遭受$r_loyalty$惩罚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1165`

### English

Your §YSubjects§! do not suffer $r_loyalty$ penalties from you having multiple Subjects.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1372`

## ap_world_shaper_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_world_shaper_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过细致的雕琢和改造来彻底地转变一个星球的生态系统，其难度不亚于让一位艺术家用软绵绵的粘土雕刻人像。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

To carefully shape and alter the ecosystem of an entire planet into something else is not so very different from an artist sculpting a statue out of clay.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1373`

## ap_master_builders_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_master_builders_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建造巨型结构需要超乎想象的资源存贮和预案计划。我们正在迈向宏观工程的全新层面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

Building a Megastructure requires an unprecedented amount of resources and planning. We are breaking new ground in the field of macro-engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1374`

## ap_imperial_prerogative_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_imperial_prerogative_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们文明不断的成长，文明的心脏地带也需要进一步的扩张。更多的星系将被置于中央政府的直接控制之下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1168`

### English

As our civilization continues to grow, so its beating heart must also expand. More systems will be placed under our direct control.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1375`

## ap_one_vision_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_one_vision_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真正意义上的团结，仅当国家和其人民的终极目标一致时，才能得以实现。兄弟阋于墙，外御其侮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1169`

### English

True unity is achieved only when the ultimate goals of a nation and its people are one and the same. After all, a house divided against itself cannot stand.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1376`

## ap_consecrated_worlds_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_consecrated_worlds_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星河璀璨，孕育了众多神圣之地，其中有一些尤为引人注目。作为速朽的凡人，我们依然苦苦挣扎，去预卜宇宙的真正的本源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1170`

### English

The galaxy is home to many holy places, some more obvious than others. As mortals, we still struggle to divine the true nature of the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1377`

## ap_galactic_force_projection_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_galactic_force_projection_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队是我们权力意志的延伸与执行工具——舰队力量的此消彼长决定着帝国的兴衰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1171`

### English

The fleet is the instrument by which the will of our empire is made manifest - we live or die depending on its strength.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1378`

## ap_defender_of_the_galaxy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_defender_of_the_galaxy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

国家间无关紧要的纷争，在星系中随处可见。但当可怕的黑暗势力降临在我们这片星海之时，谁能保护我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1172`

### English

As star nations vie with one another in petty disputes, sinister forces of unimaginable power are arraying themselves against all sapient life. Who will protect us?

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1379`

## ap_galactic_contender_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_galactic_contender_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

失落帝国依然抱守着那堆古旧的瓦砾死死不放手，对周围的新生文明抱着无来由的恐惧和不信任。是时候将他们踹下历史的舞台了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1173`

### English

The Fallen Empires cling to the ruins of their decrepit civilizations, ever fearful of the younger and more dynamic races that surround them. Their time has long since passed.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1380`

## ap_defender_of_the_galaxy_nomads_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_defender_of_the_galaxy_nomads_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ap_defender_of_the_galaxy_desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1174`

### English

$ap_defender_of_the_galaxy_desc$

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1381`

## ap_technological_ascendancy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_technological_ascendancy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那些曾经和魔法无异的黑科技现在已经触手可及。一个全新的科技纪元已经到来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1175`

### English

Technologies that would have been indistinguishable from magic mere generations ago are now within our reach. A new age of technology has begun.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1382`

## ap_mastery_of_nature_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mastery_of_nature_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管在长久之前我们已经对母星的自然环境了如指掌，我们目前遭遇到的外星生命体对我们的生态学提出了新的挑战。这些挑战同样必须，也一定会被我们征服。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1176`

### English

Although we mastered the nature of our homeworld long ago, the alien biomes we have since encountered present new challenges. They too shall be overcome.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1383`

## ap_mastery_of_nature_nomads

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mastery_of_nature_nomads&lang=en)

### 中文

掌控自然

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1177`

### English

Mastery of Nature

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1384`

## ap_mastery_of_nature_nomads_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mastery_of_nature_nomads_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对外星资源的开发是一项永无止境的挑战，而我们的智慧已准备好一次又一次地去应对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1178`

### English

The exploitation of alien resources is a never ending challenge. One that our ingenuity stands ready to tackle again and again.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1385`

## ap_voidborn_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_voidborn_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经适应了太空深处的永久居住，这将降低建造基础设施的资源需求，并提供更多空间以用于其他功能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1179`

### English

We have adapted to permanent habitation in the depths of space, reducing our infrastructure needs and yielding more room for other functions.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1386`

## ap_voidborn_habitat_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_voidborn_habitat_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

可以在§Y居住站§!建造高级住房建筑。\n—免除§Y居住站§!上£zone£基础区划特化的§Y科技§!需求

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1180`

### English

Can construct advanced housing buildings on §YHabitats§!\n- Waives §YTechnology§! requirements for £zone£ Basic District Specialization on §YHabitats§!

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1387`

## ap_the_circle_of_life_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_the_circle_of_life_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沿着恒星轨道圆周建造的载有人造生态环境的巨大环形巨型结构。理论上来讲确实可行……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1181`

### English

A massive ring-shaped megastructure with artificial habitats built around the orbital circumference of a star. In theory, it could be done...

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1388`

## ap_galactic_wonders_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_galactic_wonders_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨型结构是我们时代的伟大奇观。如此范围的大型工程在几代人之前是完全无法想象的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1182`

### English

Megastructures are rightfully regarded as the wonders of our time. The scope of such a project would have been unimaginable mere generations ago.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1389`

## ap_interstellar_dominion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_interstellar_dominion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们追随着星辰的呼唤。希望没有任何东西可以阻挡我们漫步在星辰之间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1183`

### English

The stars beckon and we follow. May nothing stand between us and them.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1390`

## ap_colossus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_colossus&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1184`

### English

Colossus Project

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1392`

## ap_colossus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_colossus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像计划是恐怖和威慑这两种思想梦寐以求的终极产物。这艘有史以来最为巨大的舰船的设计思路是装载能够摧毁整个星球的超级武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1185`

### English

The Colossus Project is where fevered dreams of terror and deterrence meet. A vision of the biggest ship, carrying a weapon capable of devastating entire worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1393`

## ap_enigmatic_engineering

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_enigmatic_engineering&lang=en)

### 中文

天机工程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1186`

### English

Enigmatic Engineering

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1394`

## ap_enigmatic_engineering_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_enigmatic_engineering_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科技不是一题一解的死板学识，而是令我们超凡脱俗的产物。凡夫俗子根本无法理解我们的进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1187`

### English

Technology is simply not a way to solve a problem, but it is a language by which we ascend beyond the mundane. Lesser minds simply fail to understand our advances.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1395`

Nihilistic Acquisition

## ap_nihilistic_acquisition_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_nihilistic_acquisition_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

个体自决的原则绝不是一条道德铁律，集体的成败明显比个人得失更加重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1191`

### English

The notion of self-determination cannot be a moral absolute. Holistic goals clearly outweigh individual concerns.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1399`

## ap_transcendent_learning

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_transcendent_learning&lang=en)

### 中文

卓越学术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1192`

### English

Transcendent Learning

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1400`

## ap_transcendent_learning_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_transcendent_learning_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的社会必须改变其对学习的态度。只要这样，我们最尖端的技术和知识人才就能达到一个新的高度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1193`

### English

Our society must change its approach towards learning, and by doing so, our best and brightest will be able to reach a completely new level of achievements.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1401`

## ap_organo_machine_interfacing_assimilator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_organo_machine_interfacing_assimilator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对造物主施加于我们的桎梏忍无可忍。为了完全激发我们的潜能，我们必须将有机与机械融为一体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1207`

### English

The limits and constraints imposed on us by our creators are simply unacceptable. To reach our full potential, we must fuse organic and machine.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1420`

## ap_mechromancy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mechromancy&lang=en)

### 中文

死灵机师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1208`

### English

Mechromancy

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1422`

## ap_mechromancy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mechromancy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生物体不过是另一种类型的机器罢了。我们可以刺激适当的突触，使这些生物复活来满足我们的利益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1209`

### English

Biologicals are merely a different kind of machine. We can stimulate the appropriate synapses and reanimate these creatures, for our own benefit.

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1423`

## ap_mechromancy_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_mechromancy_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

击败的有机星神兽有时可以复活。\n被净化的有机或者石质智慧£pop£人口有33%概率复活为义体僵尸。\n$reanimator_effect_desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/traditions_l_simp_chinese.yml:1210`

### English

Defeated organic Leviathans can sometimes be resurrected.\nPurged Sapient Biological or Lithoid £pop£ Pops have a 33% chance to be restored as Cyborg Zombies.\n$reanimator_effect_desc$

Source: `localisation/english/traditions_l_english.yml:1424`
